# Manual Simplification Audit

## Purpose

Track the manual simplification review requested for the full workspace,
including submodules. Generated and fixture-like files are inventoried but
not edited as normal code unless a later manual review proves they are stray
or safely regenerable.

## Status Legend

- Pending: not manually reviewed yet.
- Reviewed-Keep: manually reviewed and intentionally kept as-is.
- Refactor: reviewed and targeted for simplification in a checkpoint.
- Split: reviewed and targeted for extraction into smaller modules.
- Remove-Candidate: likely removable, pending proof or permission.
- Removed: removed after proof and verification.
- Fixture: test fixture, sample data, or static resource.
- Generated/Inventory Only: build, lock, generated, or runtime artifact.
- Needs Permission: larger behavior/API/workflow change needs user approval.
- Deferred: reviewed, but postponed because risk exceeds current checkpoint.
